FILE: mob/app/_layout.js

     1	import { DefaultTheme, ThemeProvider } from "@react-navigation/native";
     2	import { Stack, usePathname } from "expo-router";
     3	import { useFonts } from "expo-font";
     4	import { Animated, Platform, View } from "react-native";
     5	import { useRef } from "react";
     6	
     7	import AppHeader from "../components/AppHeader";
     8	import MainScreenPushFrame from "../components/MainScreenPushFrame";
     9	import ScreenFade from "../components/ScreenFade";
    10	import {
    11	  BackgroundHeroStateProvider,
    12	  useBackgroundHeroState,
    13	} from "../utils/backgroundHeroStateContext";
    14	import { HeaderScrollProvider } from "../utils/headerScrollContext";
    15	import {
    16	  HeaderSwipeProvider,
    17	  useHeaderSwipe,
    18	} from "../utils/headerSwipeContext";
    19	
    20	const navigationTheme = {
    21	  ...DefaultTheme,
    22	  colors: {
    23	    ...DefaultTheme.colors,
    24	    background:
    25	      Platform.OS === "web" ? "transparent" : DefaultTheme.colors.background,
    26	    card: Platform.OS === "web" ? "transparent" : DefaultTheme.colors.card,
    27	  },
    28	};
    29	
    30	function RootLayoutContent({ headerScrollY }) {
    31	  const pathname = usePathname();
    32	  const backgroundHeroState = useBackgroundHeroState();
    33	  const screenSwipe = useHeaderSwipe();
    34	
    35	  const showPersistentHeader = pathname !== "/shop";
    36	
    37	  const useOverlayHeader =
    38	    pathname === "/" ||
    39	    pathname === "/products" ||
    40	    pathname === "/aboutus" ||
    41	    pathname === "/contact";
    42	
    43	  const shouldProtectBackgroundHero =
    44	    useOverlayHeader &&
    45	    Boolean(screenSwipe?.isActive || backgroundHeroState?.isFrozen);
    46	  const backgroundHeroLayerDepth = shouldProtectBackgroundHero ? 100 : 0;
    47	
    48	  return (
    49	    <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: "#FFFCF2" }}>
    50	      {showPersistentHeader && useOverlayHeader ? (
    51	        <View
    52	          pointerEvents="none"
    53	          style={{
    54	            position: "absolute",
    55	            top: 0,
    56	            left: 0,
    57	            right: 0,
    58	            zIndex: backgroundHeroLayerDepth,
    59	            elevation: backgroundHeroLayerDepth,
    60	          }}
    61	        >
    62	          <AppHeader scrollY={headerScrollY} showOnlyHero />
    63	        </View>
    64	      ) : null}
    65	
    66	      <MainScreenPushFrame>
    67	        <Stack
    68	          screenOptions={{
    69	            headerShown: false,
    70	            animation: "none",
    71	            contentStyle: {
    72	              backgroundColor: useOverlayHeader
    73	                ? "transparent"
    74	                : "#FFFCF2",
    75	            },
    76	          }}
    77	        />
    78	      </MainScreenPushFrame>
    79	
    80	      <ScreenFade />
    81	
    82	      {showPersistentHeader && useOverlayHeader ? (
    83	        <View
    84	          pointerEvents="box-none"
    85	          style={{
    86	            position: "absolute",
    87	            top: 0,
    88	            left: 0,
    89	            right: 0,
    90	            zIndex: 1000000,
    91	            elevation: 1000000,
    92	          }}
    93	        >
    94	          <AppHeader scrollY={headerScrollY} showHero={false} />
    95	        </View>
    96	      ) : null}
    97	
    98	      {showPersistentHeader && !useOverlayHeader ? (
    99	        <AppHeader scrollY={headerScrollY} />
   100	      ) : null}
   101	    </View>
   102	  );
   103	}
   104	
   105	export default function RootLayout() {
   106	  const headerScrollY = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
   107	
   108	  const [fontsLoaded] = useFonts({
   109	    "Dream Avenue": require("../assets/fonts/dream_avenue/dream_avenue.ttf"),
   110	    "TT Fors": require("../assets/fonts/tt_fors/tt_fors_trial_regular.ttf"),
   111	  });
   112	
   113	  if (!fontsLoaded) {
   114	    return null;
   115	  }
   116	
   117	  return (
   118	    <ThemeProvider value={navigationTheme}>
   119	      <HeaderScrollProvider scrollY={headerScrollY}>
   120	        <BackgroundHeroStateProvider sourceScrollY={headerScrollY}>
   121	          <HeaderSwipeProvider>
   122	            <RootLayoutContent headerScrollY={headerScrollY} />
   123	          </HeaderSwipeProvider>
   124	        </BackgroundHeroStateProvider>
   125	      </HeaderScrollProvider>
   126	    </ThemeProvider>
   127	  );
   128	}


============================================================
FILE: mob/app/index.js
============================================================

     1	import { Animated, Image, Text, View, useWindowDimensions } from "react-native";
     2	import { LinearGradient } from "expo-linear-gradient";
     3	
     4	import CenterMagnifyView from "../components/CenterMagnifyView";
     5	import MainScreenIntroSpacer from "../components/MainScreenIntroSpacer";
     6	import PageDivider from "../components/PageDivider";
     7	import sharedStyles from "../styles/sharedStyles";
     8	import { useMainScreenScrollProps } from "../utils/mainScreenScrollContext";
     9	import useMainScreenSwipeNavigation from "../utils/useMainScreenSwipeNavigation";
    10	
    11	const passionImageBlendScrollDistance = 720;
    12	const passionImageBlendFeatherHeight = 170;
    13	const passionImageBlendOverlayColor = "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.92)";
    14	
    15	export default function HomeScreen() {
    16	  const {
    17	    compactTopLayout,
    18	    initialContentOffset,
    19	    scrollHandlers,
    20	    scrollY,
    21	  } =
    22	    useMainScreenScrollProps();
    23	  const screenSwipeHandlers = useMainScreenSwipeNavigation();
    24	  const { width: windowWidth } = useWindowDimensions();
    25	  const croppedImageWidth = windowWidth * 1.05;
    26	  const passionImageBlendTranslateY = scrollY.interpolate({
    27	    inputRange: [0, passionImageBlendScrollDistance],
    28	    outputRange: [-passionImageBlendFeatherHeight, croppedImageWidth],
    29	    extrapolate: "clamp",
    30	  });
    31	
    32	  return (
    33	    <View style={sharedStyles.screen} {...screenSwipeHandlers}>
    34	      <Animated.ScrollView
    35	        style={sharedStyles.scroll}
    36	        contentContainerStyle={sharedStyles.scrollContent}
    37	        contentOffset={initialContentOffset}
    38	        decelerationRate={0.95}
    39	        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
    40	        scrollEventThrottle={16}
    41	        {...scrollHandlers}
    42	      >
    43	        <View style={sharedStyles.main}>
    44	          <MainScreenIntroSpacer
    45	            compactTopLayout={compactTopLayout}
    46	            pageTitleStyle={sharedStyles.pageTitle}
    47	            scrollY={scrollY}
    48	          />
    49

    92	function ProductSection({ product, croppedImageWidth }) {
    93	  return (
    94	    <View style={productsStyles.productCard}>
    95	      <View style={{ width: croppedImageWidth }}>

   104	            accessibilityLabel={product.alt}
   105	          />
   106	        </View>
   107	      </View>
   108

   110	      <Text style={productsStyles.productDescription}>{product.serving}</Text>
   111	
   112	      <View style={productsStyles.productDetails}>
   113	        {product.includes.map((section) => (
   114	          <View key={section.title} style={productsStyles.includeSection}>
   115	            <View style={productsStyles.includeHeaderRow}>
   116	              <Text style={productsStyles.includeTitle}>{section.title}</Text>
   117	              <Text style={productsStyles.includeAmount}>{section.amount}</Text>
   118	            </View>
   119	
   120	            {section.items.map((item) => (
   121	              <Text key={item} style={productsStyles.includeItem}>
   122	                → {item}
   123	              </Text>
   124	            ))}
   125	
   126	            {section.note ? (
   127	              <Text style={productsStyles.includeNote}>{section.note}</Text>
   128	            ) : null}
   129	          </View>
   130	        ))}
   131	      </View>
   132	    </View>
   133	  );
   134	}

   136	export default function ProductsScreen() {
   137	  const {
   138	    compactTopLayout,
   139	    initialContentOffset,

   141	    scrollY,
   142	  } =
   143	    useMainScreenScrollProps();

   145	  const { width: windowWidth } = useWindowDimensions();
   146	  const croppedImageWidth = windowWidth * 1.05;
   147	
   148	  return (
   149	    <View style={productsStyles.screen} {...screenSwipeHandlers}>
   150	      <Animated.ScrollView
   151	        style={productsStyles.scroll}
   152	        contentContainerStyle={productsStyles.scrollContent}
   153	        contentOffset={initialContentOffset}
   154	        decelerationRate={0.95}
   155	        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
   156	        scrollEventThrottle={16}
   157	        {...scrollHandlers}

   161	            compactTopLayout={compactTopLayout}

     1	import {

    10	import { router, usePathname } from "expo-router";
    11	import { LinearGradient } from "expo-linear-gradient";
    12	import { useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
    13	
    14	import styles from "../styles/headerStyles";
    15	import { useBackgroundHeroState } from "../utils/backgroundHeroStateContext";
    16	import { useHeaderNavigationGate } from "../utils/headerNavigationGate";
    17	import {
    18	  arrowHintPeakOpacity,
    19	  useHeaderSwipe,
    20	} from "../utils/headerSwipeContext";
    21	
    22	const navPages = ["home", "products", "aboutus", "contact"];
    23	const indicatorSlideDuration = 130;
    24	const linkSlideDuration = 210;
    25	const activeTextBaseOffsetY = -3.6;
    26	const heroAnimationScrollDistance = 2000;
    27	const heroFadeScrollDistance = 480;
    28	const heroVerticalFadeScrollDistance = 720;
    29	const heroMinimumScrollOpacity = 0.1;
    30	const heroScrollFreezeProgress =
    31	  heroFadeScrollDistance / heroAnimationScrollDistance;
    32	const heroStartScale = 1.5;
    33	const heroFullScrollScale = 3.05;
    34	const heroStartTranslateY = 74;
    35	const heroFullScrollTranslateY = -56;
    36	const heroVerticalFadeHeight = 430;
    37	const heroVerticalFadeFeatherHeight = 170;
    38	const heroVerticalFadeOverlayOpacity = 1 - heroMinimumScrollOpacity;
    39	const heroVerticalFadeOverlayColor = `rgba(255, 252, 242, ${heroVerticalFadeOverlayOpacity})`;
    40	const heroScaleAtMinimumOpacity =
    41	  heroStartScale +
    42	  (heroFullScrollScale - heroStartScale) * heroScrollFreezeProgress;
    43	const heroTranslateYAtMinimumOpacity =
    44	  heroStartTranslateY +
    45	  (heroFullScrollTranslateY - heroStartTranslateY) * heroScrollFreezeProgress;
    46	const stickyExpansionMaxHeight = 20;
    47	
    48	const pageLabels = {
    49	  home: "Home",
    50	  products: "Products",
    51	  aboutus: "About Us",
    52	  contact: "Contact",
    53	  shop: "Shop",
    54	};
    55	
    56	const pageRoutes = {
    57	  home: "/",
    58	  products: "/products",
    59	  aboutus: "/aboutus",
    60	  contact: "/contact",
    61	  shop: "/shop",
    62	};
    63	
    64	function getActivePageFromPath(pathname) {
    65	  if (pathname === "/products") return "products";
    66	  if (pathname === "/aboutus") return "aboutus";
    67	  if (pathname === "/contact") return "contact";
    68	  if (pathname === "/shop") return "shop";
    69	  return "home";
    70	}
    71	
    72	function getNavigationDirection(fromPage, toPage) {
    73	  const fromIndex = navPages.indexOf(fromPage);
    74	  const toIndex = navPages.indexOf(toPage);
    75	
    76	  if (fromIndex < 0 || toIndex < 0 || fromIndex === toIndex) return 1;
    77	
    78	  const lastPageIndex = navPages.length - 1;
    79	
    80	  if (fromIndex === lastPageIndex && toIndex === 0) return 1;
    81	  if (fromIndex === 0 && toIndex === lastPageIndex) return -1;
    82	
    83	  return toIndex > fromIndex ? 1 : -1;
    84	}
    85	
    86	export default function AppHeader({
    87	  activePage,
    88	  scrollY,
    89	  showCarousel = true,
    90	  showHero = true,
    91	  showOnlyCarousel = false,
    92	  showOnlyHero = false,
    93	}) {
    94	  const pathname = usePathname();
    95	  const backgroundHeroState = useBackgroundHeroState();
    96	  const screenSwipe = useHeaderSwipe();
    97	  const resolvedActivePage = activePage || getActivePageFromPath(pathname);
    98	  const { width: windowWidth } = useWindowDimensions();
    99	  const activePageIndex = Math.max(navPages.indexOf(resolvedActivePage), 0);
   100	  const handlesCarouselVisuals = showCarousel && !showOnlyHero;
   101	
   102	  const fallbackScrollY = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
   103	  const safeScrollY = scrollY || fallbackScrollY;
   104	  const canNavigateWithHeader = useHeaderNavigationGate(safeScrollY);
   105	  const heroStateScrollY =
   106	    showHero && backgroundHeroState?.isFrozen && backgroundHeroState?.heroScrollY
   107	      ? backgroundHeroState.heroScrollY
   108	      : safeScrollY;
   109	  const headerMotionScrollY = showOnlyHero ? heroStateScrollY : safeScrollY;
   110	
   111	  const fallbackHeldArrowOpacity = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
   112	  const heldArrowOpacity =
   113	    screenSwipe?.heldArrowOpacity || fallbackHeldArrowOpacity;
   114	  const fallbackLinkTransitionProgress = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
   115	  const linkTransitionProgress =
   116	    screenSwipe?.routeTransitionProgress || fallbackLinkTransitionProgress;
   117	  const indicatorProgress = useRef(new Animated.Value(activePageIndex)).current;
   118	
   119	  const indicatorAnimationRef = useRef(null);
   120	  const linkTransitionAnimationRef = useRef(null);
   121	  const indicatorIndexRef = useRef(activePageIndex);
   122	  const visibleLinkPageRef = useRef(resolvedActivePage);
   123	  const incomingLinkPageRef = useRef(null);
   124	  const pendingLinkTransitionDirectionRef = useRef(null);
   125	  const suppressCarouselPressUntilRef = useRef(0);
   126	  const canNavigateWithHeaderRef = useRef(canNavigateWithHeader);
   127	
   128	  const activePageRef = useRef(resolvedActivePage);
   129	  const activeIndexRef = useRef(activePageIndex);
   130	  const [linkTransitionState, setLinkTransitionState] = useState({
   131	    visiblePage: resolvedActivePage,
   132	    incomingPage: null,
   133	    direction: 1,
   134	    startX: 0,
   135	  });
   136	  canNavigateWithHeaderRef.current = canNavigateWithHeader;
   137	  activePageRef.current = resolvedActivePage;
   138	  activeIndexRef.current = activePageIndex;
   139	
   140	  const showHeldArrows = () => {
   141	    if (screenSwipe?.showHeldArrowHint) {
   142	      screenSwipe.showHeldArrowHint();
   143	      return;
   144	    }
   145	
   146	    fallbackHeldArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   147	    fallbackHeldArrowOpacity.setValue(arrowHintPeakOpacity);
   148	  };
   149	
   150	  const hideHeldArrows = () => {
   151	    if (screenSwipe?.hideHeldArrowHint) {
   152	      screenSwipe.hideHeldArrowHint();
   153	      return;
   154	    }
   155	
   156	    fallbackHeldArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   157	    fallbackHeldArrowOpacity.setValue(0);
   158	  };
   159	
   160	  const updateSwipePreview = (dragDistance) => {
   161	    if (!screenSwipe) return;
   162	
   163	    screenSwipe.updateSwipe({
   164	      x: incomingLinkPageRef.current ? 0 : dragDistance,
   165	    });
   166	  };
   167	
   168	  const animateIndicatorBetweenIndexes = (fromIndex, toIndex) => {
   169	    if (fromIndex === toIndex) return;
   170	
   171	    if (indicatorAnimationRef.current) {
   172	      indicatorAnimationRef.current.stop();
   173	    }
   174	
   175	    indicatorIndexRef.current = toIndex;
   176	
   177	    const createIndicatorTiming = (toValue, duration = indicatorSlideDuration) =>
   178	      Animated.timing(indicatorProgress, {
   179	        toValue,
   180	        duration,
   181	        useNativeDriver: true,
   182	      });
   183	
   184	    const lastPageIndex = navPages.length - 1;
   185	    const animation =
   186	      fromIndex === lastPageIndex && toIndex === 0
   187	        ? Animated.sequence([
   188	            createIndicatorTiming(navPages.length),
   189	            createIndicatorTiming(-1, 0),
   190	            createIndicatorTiming(toIndex),
   191	          ])
   192	        : fromIndex === 0 && toIndex === lastPageIndex
   193	        ? Animated.sequence([
   194	            createIndicatorTiming(-1),
   195	            createIndicatorTiming(navPages.length, 0),
   196	            createIndicatorTiming(toIndex),
   197	          ])
   198	        : createIndicatorTiming(toIndex);
   199	
   200	    indicatorAnimationRef.current = animation;
   201	
   202	    animation.start(({ finished }) => {
   203	      if (finished) {
   204	        indicatorIndexRef.current = toIndex;
   205	      }
   206	
   207	      if (indicatorAnimationRef.current === animation) {
   208	        indicatorAnimationRef.current = null;
   209	      }
   210	    });
   211	  };
   212	
   213	  useEffect(() => {
   214	    animateIndicatorBetweenIndexes(indicatorIndexRef.current, activePageIndex);
   215	  }, [activePageIndex, indicatorProgress]);
   216	
   217	  useLayoutEffect(() => {
   218	    const currentVisiblePage = visibleLinkPageRef.current;
   219	
   220	    if (
   221	      currentVisiblePage === resolvedActivePage &&
   222	      !incomingLinkPageRef.current
   223	    ) {
   224	      return;
   225	    }
   226	
   227	    if (!navPages.includes(resolvedActivePage)) {
   228	      if (linkTransitionAnimationRef.current) {
   229	        linkTransitionAnimationRef.current.stop();
   230	        linkTransitionAnimationRef.current = null;
   231	      }
   232	
   233	      visibleLinkPageRef.current = resolvedActivePage;
   234	      incomingLinkPageRef.current = null;
   235	      pendingLinkTransitionDirectionRef.current = null;
   236	      if (handlesCarouselVisuals) screenSwipe?.clearSwipe();
   237	      linkTransitionProgress.setValue(0);
   238	      setLinkTransitionState({
   239	        visiblePage: resolvedActivePage,
   240	        incomingPage: null,
   241	        direction: 1,
   242	        startX: 0,
   243	      });
   244	      return;
   245	    }
   246	
   247	    const committedSwipe = handlesCarouselVisuals
   248	      ? screenSwipe?.consumeCommit(resolvedActivePage, currentVisiblePage)
   249	      : null;
   250	    const transitionDirection =
   251	      committedSwipe?.direction ||
   252	      pendingLinkTransitionDirectionRef.current ||
   253	      getNavigationDirection(currentVisiblePage, resolvedActivePage);
   254	
   255	    pendingLinkTransitionDirectionRef.current = null;
   256	
   257	    if (linkTransitionAnimationRef.current) {
   258	      linkTransitionAnimationRef.current.stop();
   259	      linkTransitionAnimationRef.current = null;
   260	    }
   261	
   262	    const transitionStartX =
   263	      committedSwipe?.x ||
   264	      (handlesCarouselVisuals ? screenSwipe?.currentXRef.current : 0) ||
   265	      0;
   266	
   267	    if (handlesCarouselVisuals && screenSwipe) {
   268	      screenSwipe.currentXRef.current = 0;
   269	    }
   270	
   271	    incomingLinkPageRef.current = resolvedActivePage;
   272	    setLinkTransitionState({
   273	      visiblePage: currentVisiblePage,
   274	      incomingPage: resolvedActivePage,
   275	      direction: transitionDirection,
   276	      startX: transitionStartX,
   277	    });
   278	    linkTransitionProgress.setValue(0);
   279	
   280	    const finishLinkTransition = ({ shouldResetProgress = false } = {}) => {
   281	      visibleLinkPageRef.current = resolvedActivePage;
   282	      incomingLinkPageRef.current = null;
   283	      if (handlesCarouselVisuals) screenSwipe?.clearSwipe();
   284	      screenSwipe?.clearCommit(committedSwipe?.id);
   285	      if (shouldResetProgress) linkTransitionProgress.setValue(0);
   286	      setLinkTransitionState({
   287	        visiblePage: resolvedActivePage,
   288	        incomingPage: null,
   289	        direction: transitionDirection,
   290	        startX: 0,
   291	      });
   292	    };
   293	
   294	    if (screenSwipe?.startRouteTransition) {
   295	      screenSwipe.startRouteTransition({
   296	        key: `${currentVisiblePage}->${resolvedActivePage}`,
   297	        duration: linkSlideDuration,
   298	        onFinish: finishLinkTransition,
   299	      });
   300	      return;
   301	    }
   302	
   303	    const animation = Animated.timing(linkTransitionProgress, {
   304	      toValue: 1,
   305	      duration: linkSlideDuration,
   306	      useNativeDriver: true,
   307	    });
   308	
   309	    linkTransitionAnimationRef.current = animation;
   310	
   311	    animation.start(({ finished }) => {
   312	      if (finished) {
   313	        finishLinkTransition({ shouldResetProgress: true });
   314	      }
   315	
   316	      if (linkTransitionAnimationRef.current === animation) {
   317	        linkTransitionAnimationRef.current = null;
   318	      }
   319	    });
   320	  }, [linkTransitionProgress, resolvedActivePage]);
   321	
   322	  const animateIndicatorToPage = (pageName) => {
   323	    const pageIndex = navPages.indexOf(pageName);
   324	    if (pageIndex < 0) return;
   325	
   326	    animateIndicatorBetweenIndexes(activeIndexRef.current, pageIndex);
   327	  };
   328	
   329	  const goToPage = (
   330	    pageName,
   331	    animateIndicator = true,
   332	    linkTransitionDirection = null
   333	  ) => {
   334	    hideHeldArrows();
   335	
   336	    if (pageName === activePageRef.current) return;
   337	
   338	    const route = pageRoutes[pageName];
   339	    if (!route) return;
   340	
   341	    if (navPages.includes(pageName)) {
   342	      if (!canNavigateWithHeaderRef.current?.()) {
   343	        screenSwipe?.clearSwipe({ animate: true });
   344	        return;
   345	      }
   346	
   347	      pendingLinkTransitionDirectionRef.current =
   348	        linkTransitionDirection ||
   349	        getNavigationDirection(activePageRef.current, pageName);
   350	
   351	      backgroundHeroState?.freezeHero(safeScrollY);
   352	      if (animateIndicator) animateIndicatorToPage(pageName);
   353	      router.replace(route);
   354	      return;
   355	    }
   356	
   357	    router.push(route);
   358	  };
   359	
   360	  const goToPreviousPage = () => {
   361	    if (Date.now() < suppressCarouselPressUntilRef.current) return;
   362	
   363	    const previousIndex =
   364	      (activeIndexRef.current + navPages.length - 1) % navPages.length;
   365	
   366	    goToPage(navPages[previousIndex], true, -1);
   367	  };
   368	
   369	  const goToNextPage = () => {
   370	    if (Date.now() < suppressCarouselPressUntilRef.current) return;
   371	
   372	    const nextIndex = (activeIndexRef.current + 1) % navPages.length;
   373	
   374	    goToPage(navPages[nextIndex], true, 1);
   375	  };
   376	
   377	  const carouselPanResponder = useRef(
   378	    PanResponder.create({
   379	      onMoveShouldSetPanResponder: (_, gestureState) =>
   380	        canNavigateWithHeaderRef.current?.() &&
   381	        Math.abs(gestureState.dx) > 7.33 &&
   382	        Math.abs(gestureState.dx) > Math.abs(gestureState.dy),
   383	
   384	      onMoveShouldSetPanResponderCapture: (_, gestureState) =>
   385	        canNavigateWithHeaderRef.current?.() &&
   386	        Math.abs(gestureState.dx) > 7.33 &&
   387	        Math.abs(gestureState.dx) > Math.abs(gestureState.dy),
   388	
   389	      onPanResponderMove: (_, gestureState) => {
   390	        if (!canNavigateWithHeaderRef.current?.()) return;
   391	
   392	        suppressCarouselPressUntilRef.current = Date.now() + 500;
   393	        updateSwipePreview(gestureState.dx);
   394	      },
   395	
   396	      onPanResponderRelease: (_, gestureState) => {
   397	        suppressCarouselPressUntilRef.current = Date.now() + 500;
   398	        hideHeldArrows();
   399	
   400	        if (!canNavigateWithHeaderRef.current?.()) {
   401	          screenSwipe?.clearSwipe({ animate: true });
   402	          return;
   403	        }
   404	
   405	        if (gestureState.dx <= -15) {
   406	          const nextIndex = (activeIndexRef.current + 1) % navPages.length;
   407	          const nextPage = navPages[nextIndex];
   408	
   409	          updateSwipePreview(gestureState.dx);
   410	          screenSwipe?.commitSwipe({
   411	            x: gestureState.dx,
   412	            page: nextPage,
   413	            direction: 1,
   414	            fromPage: activePageRef.current,
   415	          });
   416	          goToPage(nextPage, true, 1);
   417	
   418	          return;
   419	        }
   420	
   421	        if (gestureState.dx >= 15) {
   422	          const previousIndex =
   423	            (activeIndexRef.current + navPages.length - 1) % navPages.length;
   424	          const previousPage = navPages[previousIndex];
   425	
   426	          updateSwipePreview(gestureState.dx);
   427	          screenSwipe?.commitSwipe({
   428	            x: gestureState.dx,
   429	            page: previousPage,
   430	            direction: -1,
   431	            fromPage: activePageRef.current,
   432	          });
   433	          goToPage(previousPage, true, -1);
   434	
   435	          return;
   436	        }
   437	
   438	        screenSwipe?.clearSwipe({ animate: true });
   439	      },
   440	
   441	      onPanResponderTerminate: () => {
   442	        suppressCarouselPressUntilRef.current = Date.now() + 500;
   443	        hideHeldArrows();
   444	        screenSwipe?.clearSwipe({ animate: true });
   445	      },
   446	    })
   447	  ).current;
   448	
   449	  useEffect(() => {
   450	    hideHeldArrows();
   451	
   452	    return () => {
   453	      hideHeldArrows();
   454	    };
   455	  }, [resolvedActivePage, showCarousel, showOnlyHero]);
   456	
   457	  const activeLink = pageLabels[linkTransitionState.visiblePage] || "Home";
   458	  const incomingLink = linkTransitionState.incomingPage
   459	    ? pageLabels[linkTransitionState.incomingPage] || "Home"
   460	    : null;
   461	  const visibleLinkPageIndex = Math.max(
   462	    navPages.indexOf(linkTransitionState.visiblePage),
   463	    0
   464	  );
   465	  const previousDragPage =
   466	    navPages[(visibleLinkPageIndex + navPages.length - 1) % navPages.length];
   467	  const nextDragPage = navPages[(visibleLinkPageIndex + 1) % navPages.length];
   468	  const previousDragLink = pageLabels[previousDragPage] || "Home";
   469	  const nextDragLink = pageLabels[nextDragPage] || "Home";
   470	  const dragTranslateX =
   471	    handlesCarouselVisuals && screenSwipe ? screenSwipe.swipeX : 0;
   472	  const linkSlideDistance = windowWidth;
   473	  const outgoingLinkTranslateX = linkTransitionState.incomingPage
   474	    ? linkTransitionProgress.interpolate({
   475	        inputRange: [0, 1],
   476	        outputRange: [
   477	          linkTransitionState.startX,
   478	          -linkTransitionState.direction * linkSlideDistance,
   479	        ],
   480	        extrapolate: "clamp",
   481	      })
   482	    : dragTranslateX;
   483	  const arrowLinkTranslateX = outgoingLinkTranslateX;
   484	  const incomingLinkTranslateX = linkTransitionProgress.interpolate({
   485	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   486	    outputRange: [
   487	      linkTransitionState.startX +
   488	        linkTransitionState.direction * linkSlideDistance,
   489	      0,
   490	    ],
   491	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   492	  });
   493	  const previousDragTranslateX = Animated.add(
   494	    dragTranslateX,
   495	    -linkSlideDistance
   496	  );
   497	  const nextDragTranslateX = Animated.add(dragTranslateX, linkSlideDistance);
   498	
   499	  const scrollBeganArrowVisibility = headerMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   500	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   501	    outputRange: [0, 1],
   502	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   503	  });
   504	  const scrolledStateArrowOpacity = Animated.multiply(
   505	    scrollBeganArrowVisibility,
   506	    arrowHintPeakOpacity
   507	  );
   508	  const visibleArrowOpacity = Animated.add(
   509	    heldArrowOpacity,
   510	    scrolledStateArrowOpacity
   511	  );
   512	  const gatedVisibleArrowOpacity = Animated.multiply(
   513	    visibleArrowOpacity.interpolate({
   514	      inputRange: [0, arrowHintPeakOpacity],
   515	      outputRange: [0, arrowHintPeakOpacity],
   516	      extrapolate: "clamp",
   517	    }),
   518	    scrollBeganArrowVisibility
   519	  );
   520	
   521	  const heroVerticalFadeTranslateY = heroStateScrollY.interpolate({
   522	    inputRange: [0, heroVerticalFadeScrollDistance],
   523	    outputRange: [heroVerticalFadeHeight, -heroVerticalFadeFeatherHeight],
   524	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   525	  });
   526	
   527	  const stickyOffset =
   528	    Platform.OS === "web"
   529	      ? 0
   530	      : headerMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   531	          inputRange: [96, 120],
   532	          outputRange: [0, 20],
   533	          extrapolate: "clamp",
   534	        });
   535	
   536	  const centerShadowOpacity =
   537	    Platform.OS === "web"
   538	      ? 1
   539	      : headerMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   540	          inputRange: [96, 120],
   541	          outputRange: [0, 1],
   542	          extrapolate: "clamp",
   543	        });
   544	
   545	  const stickyExpansionTranslateY = headerMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   546	    inputRange: [96, 120],
   547	    outputRange: [-stickyExpansionMaxHeight, 0],
   548	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   549	  });
   550	
   551	  const heroScale = heroStateScrollY.interpolate({
   552	    inputRange: [0, heroFadeScrollDistance],
   553	    outputRange: [heroStartScale, heroScaleAtMinimumOpacity],
   554	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   555	  });
   556	
   557	  const heroTranslateY = heroStateScrollY.interpolate({
   558	    inputRange: [0, heroFadeScrollDistance],
   559	    outputRange: [heroStartTranslateY, heroTranslateYAtMinimumOpacity],
   560	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   561	  });
   562	
   563	  const headerTranslateY = headerMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   564	    inputRange: [0, 120],
   565	    outputRange: [0, -120],
   566	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   567	  });
   568	
   569	  const indicatorSegmentWidth = windowWidth / navPages.length;
   570	  const indicatorTranslateX = Animated.multiply(
   571	    indicatorProgress,
   572	    indicatorSegmentWidth
   573	  );
   574	
   575	  const handleCarouselTouchStart = () => {
   576	    showHeldArrows();
   577	    return false;
   578	  };
   579	
   580	  const sharedHeaderTouchHandlers = {
   581	    onStartShouldSetResponderCapture: handleCarouselTouchStart,
   582	    onTouchStart: showHeldArrows,
   583	    onTouchEnd: hideHeldArrows,
   584	    onTouchCancel: hideHeldArrows,
   585	  };
   586	
   587	  const carousel = (
   588	    <View
   589	      style={styles.carouselShell}
   590	      {...carouselPanResponder.panHandlers}
   591	      {...sharedHeaderTouchHandlers}
   592	    >
   593	      <Animated.View style={styles.carouselNavBar}>
   594	        <View style={styles.carouselStickyExpansion}>
   595	          <Animated.View
   596	            pointerEvents="none"
   597	            style={[
   598	              styles.carouselStickyExpansionFill,
   599	              {
   600	                transform: [{ translateY: stickyExpansionTranslateY }],
   601	              },
   602	            ]}
   603	          />
   604	        </View>
   605	
   606	        <Animated.View
   607	          style={[
   608	            styles.carouselInner,
   609	            {
   610	              transform: [{ translateY: stickyOffset }],
   611	            },
   612	          ]}
   613	        >
   614	          <Pressable
   615	            onPress={goToPreviousPage}
   616	            onPressIn={showHeldArrows}
   617	            onPressOut={hideHeldArrows}
   618	          >
   619	            <Animated.View
   620	              style={[
   621	                styles.arrowBox,
   622	                {
   623	                  opacity: gatedVisibleArrowOpacity,
   624	                  transform: [
   625	                    { translateX: arrowLinkTranslateX },
   626	                  ],
   627	                },
   628	              ]}
   629	            >
   630	              <View style={[styles.arrowChevron, styles.arrowChevronLeft]} />
   631	            </Animated.View>
   632	          </Pressable>
   633	
   634	          <Pressable
   635	            onPress={goToNextPage}
   636	            onPressIn={showHeldArrows}
   637	            onPressOut={hideHeldArrows}
   638	            style={styles.carouselActiveWrap}
   639	          >
   640	            <Animated.Text
   641	              numberOfLines={1}
   642	              adjustsFontSizeToFit
   643	              minimumFontScale={0.88}
   644	              style={[
   645	                styles.carouselActiveText,
   646	                styles.carouselActiveTextLayer,
   647	                {
   648	                  transform: [
   649	                    { translateX: outgoingLinkTranslateX },
   650	                    { translateY: activeTextBaseOffsetY },
   651	                  ],
   652	                },
   653	              ]}
   654	            >
   655	              {activeLink}
   656	            </Animated.Text>
   657	
   658	            {incomingLink ? (
   659	              <Animated.Text
   660	                numberOfLines={1}
   661	                adjustsFontSizeToFit
   662	                minimumFontScale={0.88}
   663	                style={[
   664	                  styles.carouselActiveText,
   665	                  styles.carouselActiveTextLayer,
   666	                  {
   667	                    transform: [
   668	                      { translateX: incomingLinkTranslateX },
   669	                      { translateY: activeTextBaseOffsetY },
   670	                    ],
   671	                  },
   672	                ]}
   673	              >
   674	                {incomingLink}
   675	              </Animated.Text>
   676	            ) : null}
   677	
   678	            {!incomingLink && handlesCarouselVisuals ? (
   679	              <>
   680	                <Animated.Text
   681	                  numberOfLines={1}
   682	                  adjustsFontSizeToFit
   683	                  minimumFontScale={0.88}
   684	                  style={[
   685	                    styles.carouselActiveText,
   686	                    styles.carouselActiveTextLayer,
   687	                    {
   688	                      transform: [
   689	                        { translateX: previousDragTranslateX },
   690	                        { translateY: activeTextBaseOffsetY },
   691	                      ],
   692	                    },
   693	                  ]}
   694	                >
   695	                  {previousDragLink}
   696	                </Animated.Text>
   697	
   698	                <Animated.Text
   699	                  numberOfLines={1}
   700	                  adjustsFontSizeToFit
   701	                  minimumFontScale={0.88}
   702	                  style={[
   703	                    styles.carouselActiveText,
   704	                    styles.carouselActiveTextLayer,
   705	                    {
   706	                      transform: [
   707	                        { translateX: nextDragTranslateX },
   708	                        { translateY: activeTextBaseOffsetY },
   709	                      ],
   710	                    },
   711	                  ]}
   712	                >
   713	                  {nextDragLink}
   714	                </Animated.Text>
   715	              </>
   716	            ) : null}
   717	          </Pressable>
   718	
   719	          <Pressable
   720	            onPress={goToNextPage}
   721	            onPressIn={showHeldArrows}
   722	            onPressOut={hideHeldArrows}
   723	          >
   724	            <Animated.View
   725	              style={[
   726	                styles.arrowBox,
   727	                {
   728	                  opacity: gatedVisibleArrowOpacity,
   729	                  transform: [
   730	                    { translateX: arrowLinkTranslateX },
   731	                  ],
   732	                },
   733	              ]}
   734	            >
   735	              <View style={[styles.arrowChevron, styles.arrowChevronRight]} />
   736	            </Animated.View>
   737	          </Pressable>
   738	        </Animated.View>
   739	
   740	        <Animated.View
   741	          pointerEvents="none"
   742	          style={[
   743	            styles.carouselIndicatorSeparator,
   744	            {
   745	              transform: [{ translateY: stickyOffset }],
   746	            },
   747	          ]}
   748	        />
   749	
   750	        <Animated.View
   751	          pointerEvents="none"
   752	          style={[
   753	            styles.carouselIndicatorBar,
   754	            {
   755	              transform: [{ translateY: stickyOffset }],
   756	            },
   757	          ]}
   758	        >
   759	          <View style={styles.carouselIndicatorTrack}>
   760	            {navPages.map((pageName) => (
   761	              <View
   762	                key={`carousel-indicator-track-${pageName}`}
   763	                style={styles.carouselIndicatorSegment}
   764	              />
   765	            ))}
   766	          </View>
   767	
   768	          <Animated.View
   769	            style={[
   770	              styles.carouselIndicatorActiveSegment,
   771	              {
   772	                width: indicatorSegmentWidth,
   773	                transform: [{ translateX: indicatorTranslateX }],
   774	              },
   775	            ]}
   776	          />
   777	        </Animated.View>
   778	      </Animated.View>
   779	
   780	    </View>
   781	  );
   782	
   783	  const centerShadow = (
   784	    <Animated.View
   785	      pointerEvents="none"
   786	      style={[
   787	        styles.headerCenterShadow,
   788	        {
   789	          opacity: centerShadowOpacity,
   790	          transform: [{ translateY: stickyOffset }],
   791	        },
   792	      ]}
   793	    >
   794	      {Platform.OS !== "web" && (
   795	        <>
   796	          {Array.from({ length: 36 }, (_, index) => (
   797	            <View
   798	              key={`center-shadow-layer-${index + 1}`}
   799	              style={styles[`centerShadowLayer${index + 1}`]}
   800	            />
   801	          ))}
   802	        </>
   803	      )}
   804	    </Animated.View>
   805	  );
   806	
   807	  const hero = (
   808	    <View
   809	      style={styles.hero}
   810	      {...carouselPanResponder.panHandlers}
   811	      {...sharedHeaderTouchHandlers}
   812	    >
   813	      <Animated.Image
   814	        source={require("../background1.png")}
   815	        style={[
   816	          styles.heroImage,
   817	          {
   818	            transform: [{ scale: heroScale }, { translateY: heroTranslateY }],
   819	          },
   820	        ]}
   821	        resizeMode="cover"
   822	      />
   823	      <Animated.View
   824	        pointerEvents="none"
   825	        style={[
   826	          styles.heroVerticalFadePanel,
   827	          {
   828	            transform: [{ translateY: heroVerticalFadeTranslateY }],
   829	          },
   830	        ]}
   831	      >
   832	        <LinearGradient
   833	          colors={["rgba(255, 252, 242, 0)", heroVerticalFadeOverlayColor]}
   834	          locations={[0, 1]}
   835	          style={styles.heroVerticalFadeFeather}
   836	        />
   837	        <View
   838	          style={[
   839	            styles.heroVerticalFadeSolid,
   840	            { backgroundColor: heroVerticalFadeOverlayColor },
   841	          ]}
   842	        />
   843	      </Animated.View>
   844	    </View>
   845	  );
   846	
   847	  const heroOnlyLayer = (
   848	    <Animated.View
   849	      style={[
   850	        styles.header,
   851	        {
   852	          transform: [{ translateY: headerTranslateY }],
   853	        },
   854	      ]}
   855	    >
   856	      {centerShadow}
   857	      <View style={styles.heroOnlySpacer} />
   858	      {hero}
   859	    </Animated.View>
   860	  );
   861	
   862	  if (showOnlyCarousel) return carousel;
   863	  if (showOnlyHero) return heroOnlyLayer;
   864	
   865	  return (
   866	    <Animated.View
   867	      style={[
   868	        styles.header,
   869	        {
   870	          transform: [{ translateY: headerTranslateY }],
   871	        },
   872	      ]}
   873	    >
   874	      <Animated.View style={styles.orangeBar}>
   875	        <Pressable style={styles.logoPressable} onPress={() => goToPage("home")}>
   876	          <Text style={styles.logoText}>Alla Vostra</Text>
   877	        </Pressable>
   878	
   879	        <Pressable style={styles.shopButtonWrap} onPress={() => goToPage("shop")}>
   880	          <View style={styles.shopButton}>
   881	            <Text style={styles.shopButtonText}>SHOP</Text>
   882	          </View>
   883	        </Pressable>
   884	      </Animated.View>
   885	
   886	      {showCarousel && showHero && centerShadow}
   887	      {showCarousel && carousel}
   888	      {showHero && hero}
   889	    </Animated.View>
   890	  );
   891	}


============================================================
FILE: mob/components/PageDivider.js
============================================================

     1	import { Animated } from "react-native";
     2	
     3	import sharedStyles from "../styles/sharedStyles";
     4	
     5	const topDividerFadeScrollDistance = 240;
     6	
     7	export default function PageDivider({
     8	  expandedSpacing = false,
     9	  fadeScrollDistance = topDividerFadeScrollDistance,
    10	  fadeWithScrollY = null,
    11	}) {
    12	  const fadeOpacity = fadeWithScrollY?.interpolate({
    13	    inputRange: [0, fadeScrollDistance],
    14	    outputRange: [1, 0],
    15	    extrapolate: "clamp",
    16	  });
    17	
    18	  return (
    19	    <Animated.View
    20	      style={[
    21	        sharedStyles.pageDivider,
    22	        expandedSpacing && sharedStyles.expandedPageDivider,
    23	        fadeOpacity && { opacity: fadeOpacity },
    24	      ]}
    25	    />
    26	  );
    27	}

============================================================
FILE: mob/utils/headerScrollContext.js
============================================================

     1	import { createContext, useContext } from "react";
     2	
     3	const HeaderScrollContext = createContext(null);
     4	
     5	export function HeaderScrollProvider({ children, scrollY }) {
     6	  return (
     7	    <HeaderScrollContext.Provider value={scrollY}>
     8	      {children}
     9	    </HeaderScrollContext.Provider>
    10	  );
    11	}
    12	
    13	export function useHeaderScrollY() {
    14	  return useContext(HeaderScrollContext);
    15	}

============================================================
FILE: mob/utils/openPaymentLink.js
============================================================

     1	import { Alert, Linking, Platform } from "react-native";
     2	
     3	export async function openPaymentLink(url) {
     4	  if (!url) {
     5	    if (Platform.OS === "web") {
     6	      window.alert("No payment link has been added for this product yet.");
     7	      return;
     8	    }
     9	
    10	    Alert.alert("Payment link missing", "No payment link has been added yet.");
    11	    return;
    12	  }
    13	
    14	  try {
    15	    const canOpen = await Linking.canOpenURL(url);
    16	
    17	    if (!canOpen) {
    18	      throw new Error("Cannot open URL");
    19	    }
    20	
    21	    await Linking.openURL(url);
    22	  } catch (error) {
    23	    if (Platform.OS === "web") {
    24	      window.alert("Unable to open this payment link.");
    25	      return;
    26	    }
    27	
    28	    Alert.alert("Unable to open link", "Please try again later.");
    29	  }
    30	}
